# beam-level-ladder — #104: choosing the searchlights' amplitude by measuring the layer it replaced

What this file is for. #104 swapped the launch pad's five light-show searchlights from
`CylinderGeometry(0.5, 2.6, 56, 12, 1, true)` on an opacity-driven `MeshBasicMaterial` to `UNIT_BEAM` on
`shaftMaterial` (src/world/props.js), and src/main.js's drive from `.opacity` to the shader's `uLevel`.
That swap changes what a number delivers, so the amplitude had to be re-chosen. It was chosen for
*parity with the old layer*, and parity is only a claim if the old layer's contribution was measured —
hence the legacy probe running against a pre-#104 build at the same poses with the same ruler.

Rulers (identical in both tools, so the numbers are comparable):
- clip / blown / binN = the clip sweep's own histogram over a 160x100 (16 000 px) downsample of the
  composer's output; clip% = bin7 (lum >= 224) as % of the sample, gate <= 0.5 % (tools/cdp-clip-sweep.mjs).
- touch% / mean+ / max+ = this shaft layer ALONE: the rung's frame differenced pixel-by-pixel against the
  same frame with the layer at 0; touch% = px with delta lum > 3, mean+ = their mean delta, on the
  0.2126r+0.7152g+0.0722b ruler.

Poses (three, all at sky=night / dayT=0.90). Pad datum `launchRig().pad = [-60, 0.6, -60]`, the five feet
on its own wash ring at r 13.5:
- APRON  at [-35.4, 2.4, -35.4] look [-60, 18, -60]  — 34.7 m from the pad centre, the drive-up sightline
- UP     at [-60, 2.4, -60]     look [-66, 34, -60]  — standing on the pad under the ring, worst case
- PLAZA  at [-6.8, 2.4, 10]     look [-60, 12, -60]  — the hub pad, 87.9 m away, whole-base silhouette

Byte anchor of the code being measured (taken before the runs below, verified unchanged after them):
  f8f3493cb3a87d1dce8c7ea6081ae40904c6d15319ed7597ba5aad049a30de86  src/main.js
  dd31c63d15fe141a635b7acd25a47bf56cd5fdb83e0be1790c9f9739b42da6c9  src/world/props.js
  2173d34fe1bd253f4d1b0c8576397cac48a6205cc314c2edf63e6f4bc137c2df  src/fx/beams.js
  0a35f3073afba83a484708a5d6fe5c694f072a74d4bd61950e3d715e8e804fea  tools/beam-level-ladder.mjs
  b51a45340984093d88337905ed4a31d16c02f1cf7969d80f68037f1c2d3754b2  tools/legacy-beam-layer-probe.mjs

Rig (all runs below): `python3 -m http.server 5173` over this working tree for the new build (CDP tab
:9336), and the same over /tmp/rsb-head-srv — a `git archive` mirror of commit 79775a0, i.e. the build
*before* #104 — for the legacy layer (CDP tab :9337, page `?auto=std&v=head104&demo=night`).

=== MATCHED-RESOLUTION PAIR (run A) ===
Both tabs pinned to the same drawing buffer with Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride (1810x740 CSS, dpr 1 → renderer 1303x532, i.e. the
game's own 0.72 pixel-ratio scale). This matters: touch% counts the sample cells a thin shaft survives being downsampled into, so a
1810x740 buffer reads ~1.8x the touch of a 1303x532 one for the same frame. The three tables above were taken at 1303x532 (legacy) and
1810x740 (new layer) and are NOT a matched pair; the two blocks below are.

################ FINAL BLOCK — taken on the bytes this commit ships ################
byte anchor (sha256sum, re-verified unchanged after the runs below):
  9827fb4d2c8138afccbdb4096258ae5db2dedfd3c00cbc22aa28e3975c09d809  src/main.js
  dd31c63d15fe141a635b7acd25a47bf56cd5fdb83e0be1790c9f9739b42da6c9  src/world/props.js
  2173d34fe1bd253f4d1b0c8576397cac48a6205cc314c2edf63e6f4bc137c2df  src/fx/beams.js
  0a35f3073afba83a484708a5d6fe5c694f072a74d4bd61950e3d715e8e804fea  tools/beam-level-ladder.mjs
  e10561c3cdb766d7b1fb62c27351bc94fdda8afe8a50fd9704d1d79f50da4609  tools/legacy-beam-layer-probe.mjs
  15d4851ef6fdc294ae08df481ae0457d21108be37ddb3d70d1aa31edea11c405  tools/primitive_census.mjs
both tabs pinned to drawing buffer 1303x532 (Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride 1810x740 @ deviceScaleFactor 0.72;
the new build ships 1.0 at this window and 79775a0's ships 0.72 — the override puts them on one buffer, because touch% and
the lift integrated from it are NOT resolution-robust for a thin far shaft: the same plaza frame read 0.84 at 1303 and 1.43 at 1810).
The mirror is 79775a0, several commits behind this build, so the two sides' ABSOLUTE clip baselines are not comparable
(the old plaza frame blows 0.71 % on its own — the bloom-gate work landed after it). Only the differenced layer numbers are.

================================================================================
APPENDIX — the paired-polarity rebuild, 2026-09-27 afternoon
================================================================================

RUN C above is the flaw this appendix replaces: its dark base was taken once at the start of the
ladder, and the scene's own ~2 Hz environment refresh moved the frame underneath it, so its apron
rows report a touch% that is mostly drift. Blocks D onward re-take the dark frame per rung inside
one eval (the pairing fix, described in the header of tools/beam-level-ladder.mjs), and each block
interleaves the cone layer on a mirror of the last build that had it, so both halves of the parity
claim are read from one clock:

  127.0.0.1:5174 = git archive 79775a0cb57cca2072ac2f8af71cad4aa5539338
      its src/world/props.js  30134d0aa627d6009d8628b0b77348cb55b9908c2ddf839e56133899858bc616
      its src/main.js         45b7fecd1d342d1feeda3eb87574ef48c5973b2b482fcb9d5b8705e40b5c8448
  127.0.0.1:5173 = this working tree; each block's own cache-bust is in its TARGET line
  tools/beam-level-ladder.mjs 88c3543f794af77afb25cc5de91649c8d189b896ca86c87c72c5769b16cdd1bb
  tools/legacy-beam-layer-probe.mjs e10561c3cdb766d7b1fb62c27351bc94fdda8afe8a50fd9704d1d79f50da4609
  src/world/props.js dd31c63d15fe141a635b7acd25a47bf56cd5fdb83e0be1790c9f9739b42da6c9 (same all the way through)

Between D and Q the only thing that moved in src/main.js was the comment above the amplitude being
measured, so the shaft rows of any block are the same measurement seen at a different minute; the cone
rows are the same because the mirror never changed. That sentence is not taken on trust: the anchor of
this file is the sha256 of src/main.js with full-line comments removed, which `node
tools/anchor-hash.mjs src/main.js` re-derives and `--selftest` shows moves when a digit of code moves and
holds when a comment line is added.

  ANCHOR 7b6a9e68944bd30dff6b1723f0bac1b5dce5f37665193bc667e907da69cface6  src/main.js
  ANCHOR 5f21ed5faaacf4d125bce624e011b5dce81aae4ec9563b879d551154b6f47372  src/world/props.js
  ANCHOR 66263c00844b26ba32232ddfd9e008a4ce453b52a24b80eac24c1dc84e272dbc  src/fx/beams.js

D–H and L–M also carry the whole-file sha as it stood at run time; I–K and N–P ran mid-rewrite of the
comment and record none. Q (bust show104u) is the anchored block — its whole-file sha,
eb262cb45dac877ffd26aacaa67fa6879529e138c13e3fcb0231725e0ab189c4, is on its own RUN line, and the
ANCHOR above is what the shipped file still has to match. Check:
  node tools/anchor-hash.mjs src/main.js 7b6a9e68944bd30dff6b1723f0bac1b5dce5f37665193bc667e907da69cface6
  node tools/beam-ladder-pool.mjs            # 0 unless a gate below is refused
src/world/props.js and src/fx/beams.js were not touched between D and Q at all (their BYTES shas above
are the same values the header of this file recorded).

Two readings in the blocks below are deliberate, not failures:
  - G #2 and N #1/#2 escaped the device-metrics override and drew at 1810x740. They are in the log
    because the log is append-only; the pool names them and excludes them, and pools 39 of 42 apron rows.
  - the pad-centre LADDER_RC is 1 in every block, including Q. That is the ladder refusing to call a
    frame with no light in it a measurement ("THE LAYER NEVER REACHED THIS FRAME"), which is the positive
    evidence behind the occlusion gate at the end of this file — not a run that failed.
Blocks I, J, K are ladder only (the probe was not interleaved while the comment's duplicate paragraph was
being cleared out).
